local function createHitSound(soundName, volume)
    local id = hitsounds[soundName]
    if SoulSettings.HitEffects.CustomSound ~= "" then
        id = SoulSettings.HitEffects.CustomSound
        if not string.find(id, "rbxassetid://") and not string.find(id, "http") then
            id = "rbxassetid://" .. id
        end
    end
    if not id then return end
    local s = Instance.new("Sound")
    s.SoundId = id
    s.Volume = volume or SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSoundVolume or 1
    s.Parent = Workspace
    s:Play()
    Debris:AddItem(s, 3)
end
local function CreateAtomicSlashEffect()
    local Part = Instance.new("Part")
    Part.Parent = Workspace
    Part.Transparency = 1
    Part.Anchored = true
    Part.CanCollide = false
    local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part)
    local Crescents = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Crescents.Name = "Crescents"
    Crescents.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.19, 0.38)
    Crescents.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(-360, 360)
    Crescents.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.193, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.778, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    Crescents.LightEmission = 10
    Crescents.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Crescents.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.08, 0.08)
    Crescents.Brightness = 4
    Crescents.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.4, 8.8), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
    Crescents.Rate = 999
    Crescents.Texture = "rbxassetid://4853904481"
    Crescents.Enabled = false
    local Spikes = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Spikes.Name = "Spikes"
    Spikes.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.3)
    Spikes.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(-360, 360)
    Spikes.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    Spikes.LightEmission = 10
    Spikes.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Spikes.Speed = NumberRange.new(0.08, 0.08)
    Spikes.Brightness = 5
    Spikes.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0.2, 10), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
    Spikes.Rate = 999
    Spikes.Texture = "rbxassetid://4853904481"
    Spikes.Enabled = false
    return Attachment
end
local function TriggerAtomicSlash(targetPos)
    local att = CreateAtomicSlashEffect()
    att.Parent.Position = targetPos
    for _, em in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
        if em:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            em:Emit(15)
        end
    end
    Debris:AddItem(att.Parent, 1)
end
local function CreateCoomEffect()
    local Part = Instance.new("Part")
    Part.Parent = Workspace
    Part.Transparency = 1
    Part.Anchored = true
    Part.CanCollide = false
    local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part)
    local Glow = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Glow.Name = "Glow"
    Glow.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.3, 0.6)
    Glow.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Glow.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    Glow.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 4), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 8)})
    Glow.Texture = "rbxassetid://6034177421"
    Glow.Rate = 999
    Glow.Enabled = false
    local Splash = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Splash.Name = "Splash"
    Splash.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.4, 0.8)
    Splash.Speed = NumberRange.new(10, 25)
    Splash.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(-180, 180)
    Splash.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Splash.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
    Splash.Texture = "rbxassetid://5830932079"
    Splash.Rate = 999
    Splash.Enabled = false
    return Attachment
end
local function TriggerCoom(targetPos)
    local att = CreateCoomEffect()
    att.Parent.Position = targetPos
    for _, em in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
        if em:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            em:Emit(20)
        end
    end
    Debris:AddItem(att.Parent, 1.5)
end
local function CreateCosmicExplosionEffect()
    local Part = Instance.new("Part")
    Part.Parent = Workspace
    Part.Transparency = 1
    Part.Anchored = true
    Part.CanCollide = false
    local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part)
    local Shockwave = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Shockwave.Name = "Shockwave"
    Shockwave.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.2, 0.4)
    Shockwave.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Shockwave.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    Shockwave.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 12)})
    Shockwave.Texture = "rbxassetid://5830932079"
    Shockwave.Rate = 999
    Shockwave.Enabled = false
    local Stars = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Stars.Name = "Stars"
    Stars.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1)
    Stars.Speed = NumberRange.new(5, 18)
    Stars.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(-360, 360)
    Stars.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Stars.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
    Stars.Texture = "rbxassetid://6034177421"
    Stars.Rate = 999
    Stars.Enabled = false
    return Attachment
end
local function TriggerCosmicExplosion(targetPos)
    local att = CreateCosmicExplosionEffect()
    att.Parent.Position = targetPos
    for _, em in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
        if em:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            em:Emit(25)
        end
    end
    Debris:AddItem(att.Parent, 1.5)
end
local function CreateThunderEffect()
    local Part = Instance.new("Part")
    Part.Parent = Workspace
    Part.Transparency = 1
    Part.Anchored = true
    Part.CanCollide = false
    local Attachment = Instance.new("Attachment", Part)
    local Lightning = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", Attachment)
    Lightning.Name = "Lightning"
    Lightning.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.1, 0.3)
    Lightning.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectColor)
    Lightning.Transparency = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 0), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 1)})
    Lightning.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 2), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 6)})
    Lightning.Texture = "rbxassetid://4853904481"
    Lightning.Rate = 999
    Lightning.Enabled = false
    return Attachment
end
local function TriggerThunder(targetPos)
    local att = CreateThunderEffect()
    att.Parent.Position = targetPos
    for _, em in pairs(att:GetChildren()) do
        if em:IsA("ParticleEmitter") then
            em:Emit(18)
        end
    end
    Debris:AddItem(att.Parent, 1)
end
local function TriggerHitEffect(targetPos)
    if not SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectEnabled then return end
    local effType = SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitEffectType
    if effType == "Cosmic Explosion" then
        TriggerCosmicExplosion(targetPos)
    elseif effType == "Coom" then
        TriggerCoom(targetPos)
    elseif effType == "Slash" or effType == "Atomic Slash" then
        TriggerAtomicSlash(targetPos)
    elseif effType == "Thunder" or effType == "Electric" then
        TriggerThunder(targetPos)
    else
        TriggerCosmicExplosion(targetPos)
    end
end
local function createHitSkeleton(targetChar)
    if not SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSkeleton or not targetChar then return end
    local head = targetChar:FindFirstChild("Head")
    local hrp = targetChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not head or not hrp then return end
    local line = Drawing.new("Line")
    line.Thickness = 2
    line.Color = SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSkeletonColor
    line.Transparency = 1
    line.Visible = true
    task.spawn(function()
        local startT = tick()
        while tick() - startT < 1.5 do
            local p1, on1 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position)
            local p2, on2 = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
            if on1 and on2 then
                line.From = Vector2.new(p1.X, p1.Y)
                line.To = Vector2.new(p2.X, p2.Y)
                line.Visible = true
            else
                line.Visible = false
            end
            RunService.RenderStepped:Wait()
        end
        line:Remove()
    end)
end
local function createHitChams(targetChar)
    if not SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitChams or not targetChar then return end
    local highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    highlight.FillColor = SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitChamsColor
    highlight.OutlineColor = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
    highlight.FillTransparency = SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitChamsTransparency
    highlight.OutlineTransparency = 0
    highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    highlight.Adornee = targetChar
    highlight.Parent = targetChar
    task.delay(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitChamsLifetime, function()
        pcall(function() highlight:Destroy() end)
    end)
end
local function createDamageDisplay(targetPos, damageAmount)
    if not SoulSettings.HitEffects.DamageNumbers then return end
    local part = Instance.new("Part")
    part.Transparency = 1
    part.Position = targetPos + Vector3.new(0, 1.5, 0)
    part.Anchored = true
    part.CanCollide = false
    part.Parent = Workspace
    local bb = Instance.new("BillboardGui")
    bb.Size = UDim2.new(0, 100, 0, 40)
    bb.AlwaysOnTop = true
    bb.Adornee = part
    bb.Parent = part
    local label = Instance.new("TextLabel")
    label.Size = UDim2.new(1, 0, 1, 0)
    label.BackgroundTransparency = 1
    label.Text = "-" .. tostring(math.floor(damageAmount))
    label.TextColor3 = SoulSettings.HitEffects.DamageColor
    label.TextStrokeTransparency = 0
    label.Font = Enum.Font.GothamBlack
    label.TextSize = 20
    label.Parent = bb
    local tween = TweenService:Create(part, TweenInfo.new(1, Enum.EasingStyle.Quad, Enum.EasingDirection.Out), {
        Position = part.Position + Vector3.new(0, 2.5, 0)
    })
    tween:Play()
    task.delay(1, function()
        pcall(function() part:Destroy() end)
    end)
end
local previousHealth = {}
local function monitorHealthChanges()
    for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
        if plr ~= LocalPlayer and plr.Character then
            local hum = plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            if hum then
                local lastH = previousHealth[plr.UserId] or hum.Health
                if hum.Health < lastH then
                    local diff = lastH - hum.Health
                    local hrp = plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") or plr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head")
                    if hrp then
                        if SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitNotifications then
                            StarterGui:SetCore("SendNotification", {
                                Title = "Soul.Tech Hit",
                                Text = "Hit " .. plr.DisplayName .. " for " .. tostring(math.floor(diff)) .. " DMG",
                                Duration = SoulSettings.HitEffects.NotifyTime
                            })
                        end
                        if SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSounds then
                            createHitSound(SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSoundSelect, SoulSettings.HitEffects.HitSoundVolume)
                        end
                        TriggerHitEffect(hrp.Position)
                        createHitSkeleton(plr.Character)
                        createHitChams(plr.Character)
                        createDamageDisplay(hrp.Position, diff)
                    end
                end
                previousHealth[plr.UserId] = hum.Health
            end
        end
    end
end
RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(monitorHealthChanges)
local RoadRoller = nil
local SonicRings = nil
local BlackHole = nil
local Spirit = nil
local Charm = nil
local Thanos = nil
local AfterSlash = nil
pcall(function()
    RoadRoller = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://85904556439762")[1]
    SonicRings = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://107016840921635")[1]
    BlackHole = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://107558636253769")[1]
    Spirit = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://89731535148122")[1]
    Charm = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://139067309930404")[1]
    Thanos = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://129891025243944")[1]
    AfterSlash = game:GetObjects("rbxassetid://102168430997910")[1]
end)
local lastStompTime = 0
local STOMP_COOLDOWN = 2
local function executeStompVisual(targetCharacter)
    local STOMP_EFFECT = States.StompEffect or SoulSettings.StompEffects.SelectedEffect or "Spirit"
    local currentTime = tick()
    if currentTime - lastStompTime < STOMP_COOLDOWN then return end
    lastStompTime = currentTime
    if STOMP_EFFECT == "RoadRoller" and RoadRoller then
        RoadRoller.Parent = Workspace
        RoadRoller.Anchored = true
        RoadRoller.CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 40, 0)
        local Sound = Instance.new("Sound", Workspace)
        Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://4877348549"
        Sound.PlayOnRemove = true
        Sound:Destroy()
        TweenService:Create(
            RoadRoller,
            TweenInfo.new(0.2),
            { CFrame = targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 3, 0) * CFrame.Angles(math.rad(200), -math.rad(200), math.rad(90)) }
        ):Play()
        task.wait(2)
        RoadRoller.CFrame = CFrame.new(20, 2000, 20)
    elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "Rings" and SonicRings then
        local Sound = Instance.new("Sound", Workspace)
        Sound.SoundId = "rbxassetid://7456436580"
        Sound.PlayOnRemove = true
        Sound:Destroy()
        local FakePart = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
        FakePart.Transparency = 1
        FakePart.Position = targetCharacter.Head.Position
        FakePart.Anchored = true
        FakePart.CanCollide = false
        SonicRings.Parent = FakePart
        task.wait(0.5)
        SonicRings.Parent = Workspace
        FakePart:Destroy()
    elseif STOMP_EFFECT == "BlackHole" and BlackHole then
        BlackHole.Parent = Workspace
        BlackHole.Position = targetCharacter:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso").Position + Vector3.new(0, 1, 0)
        for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do
            if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then
                emitter.Enabled = true
            end
        end
        task.wait(1.5)
        for _, emitter in pairs(BlackHole:GetDescendants()) do
            if emitter:IsA("ParticleEmitter") or emitter:IsA("Beam") then
                emitter.Enabled = false
            end
        end
    else
        TriggerCosmicExplosion(targetCharacter.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
    end
end
function Modules:StompEffects(state)
    if not state then
        DhLibrary:RemoveConnection("StompEffect")
        return
    end
    DhLibrary:AddConnection("StompEffect", RunService.Heartbeat:Connect(function()
        local playerChar = LocalPlayer.Character
        if not playerChar or not playerChar:FindFirstChild("LowerTorso") or not playerChar:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") then
            return
        end
        local rayOrigin = playerChar.LowerTorso.Position
        local rayDirection = Vector3.new(0, -playerChar.UpperTorso.Size.Y * 4.5, 0)
        local whitelist = {}
        for _, player in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
            if player ~= LocalPlayer and player.Character then
                table.insert(whitelist, player.Character)
            end
        end
        if #whitelist > 0 then
            local hitPart, hitPosition = Workspace:FindPartOnRayWithWhitelist(Ray.new(rayOrigin, rayDirection), whitelist, false, true)
            if hitPart then
                local stompedChar = hitPart:FindFirstAncestorOfClass("Model")
                if stompedChar and stompedChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and isPlayerKnocked(Players:GetPlayerFromCharacter(stompedChar)) then
                    executeStompVisual(stompedChar)
                end
            end
        end
    end))
end
local ESPObjects = {}
local function createESPObject(player)
    local esp = {
        Player = player,
        Box = Drawing.new("Square"),
        BoxOutline = Drawing.new("Square"),
        BoxFill = Drawing.new("Square"),
        Ring = Drawing.new("Circle"),
        Name = Drawing.new("Text"),
        Distance = Drawing.new("Text"),
        HealthBar = Drawing.new("Line"),
        HealthBarOutline = Drawing.new("Line"),
        HealthText = Drawing.new("Text"),
        Tracer = Drawing.new("Line"),
        Skeleton = {
            Head = Drawing.new("Line"),
            Spine = Drawing.new("Line"),
            LeftArm = Drawing.new("Line"),
            RightArm = Drawing.new("Line"),
            LeftLeg = Drawing.new("Line"),
            RightLeg = Drawing.new("Line")
        },
        Highlight = Instance.new("Highlight")
    }
    esp.Box.Thickness = 1.5
    esp.Box.Filled = false
    esp.Box.Visible = false
    esp.BoxOutline.Thickness = 3
    esp.BoxOutline.Filled = false
    esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
    esp.BoxFill.Filled = true
    esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
    esp.Ring.Thickness = 1.5
    esp.Ring.NumSides = 24
    esp.Ring.Filled = false
    esp.Ring.Visible = false
    esp.Name.Size = 13
    esp.Name.Center = true
    esp.Name.Outline = true
    esp.Name.Font = 2
    esp.Name.Visible = false
    esp.Distance.Size = 11
    esp.Distance.Center = true
    esp.Distance.Outline = true
    esp.Distance.Font = 2
    esp.Distance.Visible = false
    esp.HealthBar.Thickness = 2.5
    esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Thickness = 4.5
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Color = Color3.fromRGB(0, 0, 0)
    esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
    esp.HealthText.Size = 11
    esp.HealthText.Center = false
    esp.HealthText.Outline = true
    esp.HealthText.Font = 2
    esp.HealthText.Visible = false
    esp.Tracer.Thickness = 1.5
    esp.Tracer.Visible = false
    for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do
        bone.Thickness = 1.5
        bone.Visible = false
    end
    esp.Highlight.DepthMode = Enum.HighlightDepthMode.AlwaysOnTop
    esp.Highlight.Enabled = false
    ESPObjects[player] = esp
end
local function removeESPObject(player)
    local esp = ESPObjects[player]
    if esp then
        pcall(function() esp.Box:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.BoxOutline:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.BoxFill:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Ring:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Name:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Distance:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthBar:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthBarOutline:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.HealthText:Remove() end)
        pcall(function() esp.Tracer:Remove() end)
        for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do
            pcall(function() bone:Remove() end)
        end
        pcall(function() esp.Highlight:Destroy() end)
        ESPObjects[player] = nil
    end
end
for _, plr in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
        createESPObject(plr)
    end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(plr)
    if plr ~= LocalPlayer then
        createESPObject(plr)
    end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(removeESPObject)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    for plr, esp in pairs(ESPObjects) do
        if plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") and plr.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid") then
            local char = plr.Character
            local hrp = char.HumanoidRootPart
            local hum = char:FindFirstChildOfClass("Humanoid")
            local head = char:FindFirstChild("Head")
            local dist = (Camera.CFrame.Position - hrp.Position).Magnitude
            if dist > SoulSettings.ESP.MaxDistance or (SoulSettings.ESP.TeamCheck and plr.Team == LocalPlayer.Team) or hum.Health <= 0 then
                esp.Box.Visible = false
                esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
                esp.Ring.Visible = false
                esp.Name.Visible = false
                esp.Distance.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do bone.Visible = false end
                esp.Highlight.Enabled = false
                do end
            end
            local hrpPos, onScreen = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position)
            if onScreen then
                local headPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(head.Position + Vector3.new(0, 0.5, 0))
                local legPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 3, 0))
                local boxHeight = math.abs(headPos.Y - legPos.Y)
                local boxWidth = boxHeight * 0.65
                local boxTopLeft = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X - boxWidth / 2, headPos.Y)
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Box then
                    esp.Box.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, boxHeight)
                    esp.Box.Position = boxTopLeft
                    esp.Box.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.BoxColor
                    esp.Box.Visible = true
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.BoxOutline then
                        esp.BoxOutline.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, boxHeight)
                        esp.BoxOutline.Position = boxTopLeft
                        esp.BoxOutline.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.BoxOutlineColor
                        esp.BoxOutline.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                    end
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.BoxGradient then
                        esp.BoxFill.Size = Vector2.new(boxWidth, boxHeight)
                        esp.BoxFill.Position = boxTopLeft
                        esp.BoxFill.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.BoxGradientColor1
                        esp.BoxFill.Transparency = 1 - SoulSettings.ESP.BoxFillTransparency
                        esp.BoxFill.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    esp.Box.Visible = false
                    esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                    esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Ring then
                    local bottomPos = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(hrp.Position - Vector3.new(0, 2.8, 0))
                    esp.Ring.Position = Vector2.new(bottomPos.X, bottomPos.Y)
                    esp.Ring.Radius = math.clamp(boxWidth * 0.5, 5, 50)
                    esp.Ring.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.RingColor
                    esp.Ring.Visible = true
                else
                    esp.Ring.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Name then
                    esp.Name.Position = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, headPos.Y - 16)
                    esp.Name.Text = plr.DisplayName .. " (@" .. plr.Name .. ")"
                    esp.Name.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255, 255, 255)
                    esp.Name.Visible = true
                else
                    esp.Name.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Distance then
                    esp.Distance.Position = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, legPos.Y + 2)
                    esp.Distance.Text = tostring(math.floor(dist)) .. " studs"
                    esp.Distance.Color = Color3.fromRGB(200, 200, 200)
                    esp.Distance.Visible = true
                else
                    esp.Distance.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.HealthBar then
                    local hpPercent = math.clamp(hum.Health / hum.MaxHealth, 0, 1)
                    local barHeight = boxHeight * hpPercent
                    local barX = boxTopLeft.X - 6
                    esp.HealthBarOutline.From = Vector2.new(barX, boxTopLeft.Y)
                    esp.HealthBarOutline.To = Vector2.new(barX, boxTopLeft.Y + boxHeight)
                    esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = true
                    esp.HealthBar.From = Vector2.new(barX, boxTopLeft.Y + (boxHeight - barHeight))
                    esp.HealthBar.To = Vector2.new(barX, boxTopLeft.Y + boxHeight)
                    esp.HealthBar.Color = Color3.fromRGB(255 * (1 - hpPercent), 255 * hpPercent, 50)
                    esp.HealthBar.Visible = true
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.HealthText then
                        esp.HealthText.Position = Vector2.new(barX - 25, boxTopLeft.Y + (boxHeight - barHeight) - 4)
                        esp.HealthText.Text = tostring(math.floor(hum.Health))
                        esp.HealthText.Color = esp.HealthBar.Color
                        esp.HealthText.Visible = true
                    else
                        esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                    end
                else
                    esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                    esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                    esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Tracer then
                    local origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y)
                    if SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Top" then
                        origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, 0)
                    elseif SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Center" then
                        origin = Vector2.new(Camera.ViewportSize.X / 2, Camera.ViewportSize.Y / 2)
                    elseif SoulSettings.ESP.TracerOrigin == "Mouse" then
                        origin = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
                    end
                    esp.Tracer.From = origin
                    esp.Tracer.To = Vector2.new(hrpPos.X, legPos.Y)
                    esp.Tracer.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.TracerColor
                    esp.Tracer.Visible = true
                else
                    esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                end
                if SoulSettings.ESP.Chams then
                    esp.Highlight.Adornee = char
                    esp.Highlight.Parent = char
                    esp.Highlight.FillColor = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamsColor
                    esp.Highlight.OutlineColor = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamsOutlineColor
                    esp.Highlight.FillTransparency = SoulSettings.ESP.ChamFillTransparency
                    esp.Highlight.Enabled = true
                else
                    esp.Highlight.Enabled = false
                end
            else
                esp.Box.Visible = false
                esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
                esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
                esp.Ring.Visible = false
                esp.Name.Visible = false
                esp.Distance.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
                esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
                esp.HealthText.Visible = false
                esp.Tracer.Visible = false
                for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do bone.Visible = false end
                esp.Highlight.Enabled = false
            end
        else
            esp.Box.Visible = false
            esp.BoxOutline.Visible = false
            esp.BoxFill.Visible = false
            esp.Ring.Visible = false
            esp.Name.Visible = false
            esp.Distance.Visible = false
            esp.HealthBar.Visible = false
            esp.HealthBarOutline.Visible = false
            esp.HealthText.Visible = false
            esp.Tracer.Visible = false
            for _, bone in pairs(esp.Skeleton) do bone.Visible = false end
            esp.Highlight.Enabled = false
        end
    end
end)
local function SetupAura(char)
    if not SoulSettings.ESP.SafeAura or not char then return end
    local torso = char:FindFirstChild("UpperTorso") or char:FindFirstChild("Torso")
    if not torso then return end
    local folder = torso:FindFirstChild("AuraSafe")
    if folder then folder:Destroy() end
    folder = Instance.new("Folder")
    folder.Name = "AuraSafe"
    folder.Parent = torso
    local pointLight = Instance.new("PointLight", folder)
    pointLight.Range = 8
    pointLight.Brightness = 3
    pointLight.Color = SoulSettings.ESP.SafeColor
    local att = Instance.new("Attachment", folder)
    local emitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
    emitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.ESP.SafeColor)
    emitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(0.5, 1)
    emitter.Rate = 20
    emitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(1, 3)
    emitter.Size = NumberSequence.new({NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(0, 1), NumberSequenceKeypoint.new(1, 0)})
    emitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://5830932079"
end
LocalPlayer.CharacterAdded:Connect(function(char)
    task.wait(0.5)
    SetupAura(char)
end)
local BulletTextures = {
    ["Electric"] = "rbxassetid://4853904481",
    ["Laser"] = "rbxassetid://5830932079",
    ["Rainbow"] = "rbxassetid://6034177421",
    ["Smoke"] = "rbxassetid://6034177421",
    ["Star"] = "rbxassetid://6034177421",
    ["Fire"] = "rbxassetid://4853904481"
}
local function CreateBulletTracer(fromPos, toPos)
    if not SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracers then return end
    local p1 = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
    p1.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
    p1.Transparency = 1
    p1.Anchored = true
    p1.CanCollide = false
    p1.Position = fromPos
    local p2 = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
    p2.Size = Vector3.new(0.1, 0.1, 0.1)
    p2.Transparency = 1
    p2.Anchored = true
    p2.CanCollide = false
    p2.Position = toPos
    local a1 = Instance.new("Attachment", p1)
    local a2 = Instance.new("Attachment", p2)
    local beam = Instance.new("Beam", p1)
    beam.Attachment0 = a1
    beam.Attachment1 = a2
    beam.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersColor)
    beam.Width0 = SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersSize * 0.1
    beam.Width1 = SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersSize * 0.1
    beam.Transparency = NumberSequence.new(SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersTransparency)
    beam.Texture = BulletTextures[SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersTexture] or "rbxassetid://4853904481"
    beam.TextureSpeed = 2
    task.delay(SoulSettings.ESP.BulletTracersTimeAlive, function()
        pcall(function()
            p1:Destroy()
            p2:Destroy()
        end)
    end)
end
local crosshairLines = {
    Top = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Bottom = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Left = Drawing.new("Line"),
    Right = Drawing.new("Line")
}
local crosshairText = Drawing.new("Text")
crosshairText.Size = 13
crosshairText.Center = true
crosshairText.Outline = true
crosshairText.Font = 2
crosshairText.Text = "Soul.Tech"
local crosshairAngle = 0
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not SoulSettings.Crosshair.Enabled then
        for _, l in pairs(crosshairLines) do l.Visible = false end
        crosshairText.Visible = false
        return
    end
    local center = UserInputService:GetMouseLocation()
    if SoulSettings.Crosshair.FollowTarget and TargetPlr and TargetPlr.Character and TargetPlr.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        local sp, on = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(TargetPlr.Character.Head.Position)
        if on then center = Vector2.new(sp.X, sp.Y) end
    end
    crosshairAngle = crosshairAngle + (SoulSettings.Crosshair.SpinSpeed * 2)
    local rad = math.rad(crosshairAngle)
    local len = SoulSettings.Crosshair.LineLength
    local gap = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Gap
    local col = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Color
    if SoulSettings.Crosshair.Rainbow then
        col = Color3.fromHSV((tick() * 0.5) % 1, 0.8, 1)
    end
    local angles = { 0, 90, 180, 270 }
    local names = { "Right", "Bottom", "Left", "Top" }
    for i = 1, 4 do
        local a = math.rad(crosshairAngle + angles[i])
        local dir = Vector2.new(math.cos(a), math.sin(a))
        local line = crosshairLines[names[i]]
        line.From = center + dir * gap
        line.To = center + dir * (gap + len)
        line.Color = col
        line.Thickness = SoulSettings.Crosshair.Thickness
        line.Visible = true
    end
    if SoulSettings.Crosshair.ShowText then
        crosshairText.Position = center + Vector2.new(0, gap + len + 4)
        crosshairText.Color = col
        crosshairText.Visible = true
    else
        crosshairText.Visible = false
    end
end)
local rainPart = nil
local rainEmitter = nil
local function updateRain()
    if SoulSettings.Rain.Enabled then
        if not rainPart then
            rainPart = Instance.new("Part")
            rainPart.Size = Vector3.new(100, 1, 100)
            rainPart.Transparency = 1
            rainPart.Anchored = true
            rainPart.CanCollide = false
            rainPart.Parent = Workspace
            local att = Instance.new("Attachment", rainPart)
            rainEmitter = Instance.new("ParticleEmitter", att)
            rainEmitter.Texture = "rbxassetid://4853904481"
            rainEmitter.Speed = NumberRange.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Speed, SoulSettings.Rain.Speed * 1.5)
            rainEmitter.Lifetime = NumberRange.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Lifetime, SoulSettings.Rain.Lifetime * 1.2)
            rainEmitter.Rate = SoulSettings.Rain.Rate
            rainEmitter.Color = ColorSequence.new(SoulSettings.Rain.Color)
            rainEmitter.EmissionDirection = Enum.NormalId.Bottom
            rainEmitter.SpreadAngle = Vector2.new(10, 10)
        end
        rainPart.CFrame = Camera.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, 30, 0)
    else
        if rainPart then
            rainPart:Destroy()
            rainPart = nil
            rainEmitter = nil
        end
    end
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(updateRain)
local chinaHatMesh = nil
local chinaHatLight = nil
local function updateChinaHat()
    if SoulSettings.ChinaHat.Enabled and LocalPlayer.Character and LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("Head") then
        local head = LocalPlayer.Character.Head
        if not chinaHatMesh or not chinaHatMesh.Parent then
            chinaHatMesh = Instance.new("SpecialMesh")
            local part = Instance.new("Part", Workspace)
            part.Name = "SoulTech_ChinaHat"
            part.CanCollide = false
            part.Anchored = true
            part.CastShadow = false
            chinaHatMesh.MeshId = "rbxassetid://1033714"
            chinaHatMesh.TextureId = ""
            chinaHatMesh.Parent = part
            chinaHatLight = Instance.new("PointLight", part)
        end
        local part = chinaHatMesh.Parent
        chinaHatMesh.Scale = Vector3.new(SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleX, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleY, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.ScaleZ)
        part.Color = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.Color
        chinaHatLight.Color = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightColor
        chinaHatLight.Brightness = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightBrightness
        chinaHatLight.Range = SoulSettings.ChinaHat.LightRange
        local rot = (tick() * SoulSettings.ChinaHat.RotationSpeed * 60) % 360
        part.CFrame = head.CFrame * CFrame.new(0, SoulSettings.ChinaHat.HoverOffset, 0) * CFrame.Angles(0, math.rad(rot), 0)
    else
        if chinaHatMesh and chinaHatMesh.Parent then
            chinaHatMesh.Parent:Destroy()
            chinaHatMesh = nil
            chinaHatLight = nil
        end
    end
end
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(updateChinaHat)
local function NewRadarCircle(transparency, color, radius, filled, thickness)
    local c = Drawing.new("Circle")
    c.Transparency = transparency
    c.Color = color
    c.Visible = false
    c.Thickness = thickness
    c.Position = Vector2.new(0, 0)
    c.Radius = radius
    c.NumSides = math.clamp(math.floor(radius * 0.55), 10, 75)
    c.Filled = filled
    return c
end
local RadarBackground = NewRadarCircle(0.9, SoulSettings.Radar.BackgroundColor, SoulSettings.Radar.Radius, true, 1)
local RadarBorder = NewRadarCircle(0.75, SoulSettings.Radar.BorderColor, SoulSettings.Radar.Radius, false, 2)
local RadarCenter = NewRadarCircle(1, SoulSettings.Radar.LocalDotColor, 3, true, 1)
local RadarDots = {}
local function CreateRadarDot(plr)
    local dot = NewRadarCircle(1, SoulSettings.Radar.PlayerDotColor, 3, true, 1)
    RadarDots[plr] = dot
end
local function RemoveRadarDot(plr)
    if RadarDots[plr] then
        RadarDots[plr]:Remove()
        RadarDots[plr] = nil
    end
end
for _, v in ipairs(Players:GetPlayers()) do
    if v ~= LocalPlayer then CreateRadarDot(v) end
end
Players.PlayerAdded:Connect(function(v)
    if v ~= LocalPlayer then CreateRadarDot(v) end
end)
Players.PlayerRemoving:Connect(RemoveRadarDot)
RunService.RenderStepped:Connect(function()
    if not SoulSettings.Radar.Enabled then
        RadarBackground.Visible = false
        RadarBorder.Visible = false
        RadarCenter.Visible = false
        for _, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do dot.Visible = false end
        return
    end
    local center = SoulSettings.Radar.Position
    local rad = SoulSettings.Radar.Radius
    RadarBackground.Position = center
    RadarBackground.Radius = rad
    RadarBackground.Color = SoulSettings.Radar.BackgroundColor
    RadarBackground.Visible = true
    RadarBorder.Position = center
    RadarBorder.Radius = rad
    RadarBorder.Color = SoulSettings.Radar.BorderColor
    RadarBorder.Visible = true
    RadarCenter.Position = center
    RadarCenter.Color = SoulSettings.Radar.LocalDotColor
    RadarCenter.Visible = true
    local myChar = LocalPlayer.Character
    local myHRP = myChar and myChar:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
    if not myHRP then return end
    for plr, dot in pairs(RadarDots) do
        if plr and plr.Character and plr.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart") then
            local enemyHRP = plr.Character.HumanoidRootPart
            local diff = enemyHRP.Position - myHRP.Position
            local camCF = Camera.CFrame
            local relX = diff:Dot(camCF.RightVector)
            local relZ = -diff:Dot(camCF.LookVector)
            local scale = rad / SoulSettings.Radar.Range
            local screenX = center.X + (relX * scale)
            local screenY = center.Y + (relZ * scale)
            local offsetDist = (Vector2.new(screenX, screenY) - center).Magnitude
            if offsetDist <= rad - 2 then
                dot.Position = Vector2.new(screenX, screenY)
                dot.Color = SoulSettings.Radar.PlayerDotColor
                dot.Visible = true
            else
                dot.Visible = false
            end
        else
            dot.Visible = false
        end
    end
end)
end
do
function RunPredictAutoArmor(dt)
		local target = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target)
			or (Settings.Misc.Players.AutoKill.Enabled and Settings.Misc.Players.Target)
			or nil
		if not target or not LocalPlayer.Character then return end
		local lhrp = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not lhrp then return end
		if LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildOfClass("ForceField") then return end
		local char = target.Character
		local hrp  = char and char:FindFirstChild("HumanoidRootPart")
		if not hrp or (hrp.Position - lhrp.Position).Magnitude <= 25.1 then return end
		local rpg = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
		if not rpg then return end
		local lastPos       = hrp.Position
		local isInPosition  = nil
		local shopArmorData = workspace:FindFirstChild("Shops")
		if shopArmorData then
			for _, shopModel in ipairs(shopArmorData:GetChildren()) do
				local button = shopModel:FindFirstChild("Button") or shopModel:FindFirstChild("button")
				if button then
					if (lastPos - button.Position).Magnitude <= 9 then
						isInPosition = button.Position
						break
					end
				end
			end
		end
		local targetBodyEffects = char:FindFirstChild("BodyEffects")
		local targetFF = targetBodyEffects and targetBodyEffects:FindFirstChild("ForceField") and targetBodyEffects.ForceField.Value
		if targetFF then
			isInPosition = nil
		end
		local ignored = workspace:FindFirstChild("Ignored")
		local has_one = false
		if ignored then
			for _, instance in ipairs(ignored:GetChildren()) do
				if instance.Name == "Model" then
					local part = instance:FindFirstChild("Launcher")
					if part then
						local ok, owned = pcall(isnetworkowner, part)
						if ok and owned then
							has_one = true
						end
						local bv = part:FindFirstChild("BodyVelocity")
						if bv then bv:Destroy() end
						local ti = part:FindFirstChild("TouchInterest")
						if ti then ti:Destroy() end
						if isInPosition then
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(isInPosition + NewVector3(0, math.random(-185, -150) / 100, 0))
						else
							local aimPos = (Settings.Ragebot.General.Enabled and Table.Ragebot.Target and Ragebot.GetPredictedPosition())
								or (hrp.Position)
							part.CFrame = CFrame.new(aimPos + NewVector3(0, 100, 0))
						end
						part.Velocity = NewVector3(0, 1.85, 0)
					end
				end
			end
		end
		if not has_one and PredictAutoArmorReady and not targetFF then
			PredictAutoArmorReady = false
			task.spawn(function()
				local oldTool = LocalPlayer.Character:FindFirstChildWhichIsA("Tool")
				if oldTool then
					oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					task.wait(2)
				end
				local rpgRef = LocalPlayer.Backpack:FindFirstChild("[RPG]")
				if rpgRef then
					rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					task.wait(0.03)
					if rpgRef.Parent == LocalPlayer.Character then
						rpgRef:Activate()
					end
					task.delay(2.5, function()
						PredictAutoArmorReady = true
					end)
					task.wait(0.03)
					if MainEvent then
						MainEvent:FireServer("Reload", rpgRef)
					end
					if rpgRef and rpgRef.Parent then
						rpgRef.Parent = LocalPlayer.Backpack
					end
					task.wait(0.03)
					if oldTool and oldTool.Parent == LocalPlayer.Backpack then
						oldTool.Parent = LocalPlayer.Character
					end
				else
					PredictAutoArmorReady = true
				end
			end)
		end
	end
	Utility.NewConnection(RunService.Heartbeat, LPH_NO_VIRTUALIZE(function(dt)
		if Settings.Misc.Other.PredictAutoArmor.Enabled then
			pcall(RunPredictAutoArmor, dt)
		end
	end))
	local SilentAim = {} do
	    SilentAim.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.SilentAim.Target, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Root" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Head" and Table.SilentAim.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.SilentAim.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and InAir then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.Humanization.Value * 0.01)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.SilentAim.Target) then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	    local RoundMultiple = function(...)
            local A = {}
            for I, V in next, table.pack(...) do
                A[I] = Round(V)
            end
            return unpack(A)
        end
        local WorldToViewport = function(...)
            local A, B = Camera:WorldToViewportPoint(...)
            return NewVector2(A.X, A.Y), B, A.Z
        end
        SilentAim.BoxFov = function(Player)
            local Character = Player and Player.Character
            if Character then
                local CFrame = Character:GetModelCFrame()
                local Position, Visible, Depth = WorldToViewport(Character.HumanoidRootPart.Position)
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Enabled and Visible
                if Visible then
                    local ScaleFactor = 1 / (Depth * Tan(Rad(Camera.FieldOfView / 2)) * 2) * 1000
                    local Width, Height = RoundMultiple(4 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum, 7 * ScaleFactor * Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.Maximum)
                    local X, Y = RoundMultiple(Position.X, Position.Y)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size = NewVector2(Width, Height)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position = NewVector2(RoundMultiple(X - Width / 2, Y - Height / 2))
                    Drawings.SilentAimBox.Color = Settings.Legitbot.SilentAim.AntiCurve.ShowBox.Color
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Size = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Size - NewVector2(2, 2)
                    Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Position = Drawings.SilentAimBox.Position + NewVector2(1, 1)
                end
            else
                Drawings.SilentAimBox.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxOutline.Visible = false
                Drawings.SilentAimBoxInline.Visible = false
            end
        end
	end
	local AimAssist = {} do
	    AimAssist.MouseMoveRel = function(Position, Smoothing)
		    local MousePosition = UserInputServive:GetMouseLocation()
	    	mousemoverel((Position.X - MousePosition.X) / Smoothing, (Position.Y - MousePosition.Y) / Smoothing)
	    end
	    AimAssist.GetPredictedPosition = function()
	        local Velocity = Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Enabled and Combat.CalculateResolverOffset(Table.AimAssist.Target, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.Method, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Resolver.UpdateTime) or Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart.Velocity
            local HitPart = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Upper Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.UpperTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Lower Torso" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.LowerTorso) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Root" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.HumanoidRootPart) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Head" and Table.AimAssist.Target.Character.Head)
            local Position = (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Part" and Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target).Position) or (Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.HitPart == "Closest Point" and Combat.GetClosestPoint(Combat.GetClosestPart(Table.AimAssist.Target))) or (HitPart.Position)
            if not Position then return end
            local Ping = Table.Ping / 500
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.Enabled and Utility.IsInAir(Table.AimAssist.Target) then
                Velocity = NewVector3(Velocity.X, Abs(Velocity.Y * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.AntiGroundShots.ToTakeOff), Velocity.Z)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Custom then
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Prediction.Amount
            else
                PredictedPosition = Position + Velocity * Ping
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Enabled then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + CustomMath.RandomVector3(Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.Shake.Amount)
            end
            if Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Enabled and InAir then
                PredictedPosition = PredictedPosition + NewVector3(0, Settings.Legitbot.AimAssist.JumpOffset.Offset, 0)
            end
            return PredictedPosition
	    end
	end
